# Deep acyclic call graph

> [!CAUTION]
> **Executable Conformance Test Markdown**
>
> - This file controls executable conformance tests; it is not free-form documentation.
> - Keep its structural elements in the form required by the Conformance Test Markdown syntax.
> - Validate every edit with a conforming parser and runner.

This suite verifies resource analysis of a valid call graph independently of the native implementation stack. It compiles without executing the deep chain.

---

## Test: ten thousand nested calls compile without native stack exhaustion

This case requires iterative or otherwise stack-safe resource analysis while remaining within the portable bytecode limits.

### Case description

```yaml
gesBlock: case
id: ten-thousand-calls
kind: compileMetadata
level: scenario
sources:
  - name: ten-thousand-calls.ges
    program: main
```
